| `PricingSnapshot` | value object | exactly `catalog_id: PricingCatalogId`, `version >= 1`, `content_digest: ContentDigest`, `entry_ids: list[PriceEntryId]` (non-empty, P, N51), all required. `digest` is an unknown field (O6) |
| `CostRounding` | value object | exactly `scale` (the integer 18), `mode` (half_even; an `enum`), `exact` (the boolean false), all three required |
| `ReportedCostRaw` | value object | `reported_by` (provider, telemetry_platform), `amount_raw: VerbatimText` (kept as sent, never parsed), `currency_raw?: VerbatimText`, `currency_source` (payload, api_contract, none). `currency_raw` is present iff `currency_source` is not none; the error is at `currency_raw` |
| `CostRecord` (`cst`) | entity, record of fact | `domain`, `usage_record_ids: list[UsageRecordId]`, `status` (available, not_available), `amount?: Money`, `basis?` (reported, computed), `reported_by?`, `pricing_snapshot?`, `rounding?`, `na_reason?` (the eight reasons), `unpriced_units: list[unit]` (default `[]`), `reported_raw?`. Rules, each located at the field it is about: available needs `amount` and `basis` and has no `na_reason`; `amount` >= 0 (zero accepted); basis reported needs `reported_by` and has no `pricing_snapshot` and no `rounding` (P, N51); basis computed needs `pricing_snapshot` and has no `reported_by` (P, N51); not_available needs `na_reason` and has no `amount` (not even "0"), `basis`, `reported_by`, `pricing_snapshot` or `rounding`; `reported_raw` is present iff `na_reason` is unsupported_currency or currency_not_reported, with `currency_not_reported` going with `currency_source=none` and `unsupported_currency` with payload or api_contract; `unpriced_units` is non-empty iff `na_reason=unit_unpriced`, each unit listed once (P, N51) |
| `CostExclusion` | value object | exactly `ref: CostRecordId`, `reason` (the eight reasons and duplicate_of_reported; an `enum`), `covered_by: list[CostRecordId]`. `duplicate_of_reported` needs a non-empty `covered_by` (error at `covered_by`). (rev 20, N78) `covered_by` names each record at most once: a repeat is rejected at `covered_by` (`uniqueItems`) |
| `CostSummary` (`csum`) | entity, record of fact | `domain`, `total?: Money`, `included: list[CostRecordId]`, `excluded: list[CostExclusion]`, `completeness` (complete, partial, none). No `totals`. Rules on the summary alone: `included` and the `ref`s of `excluded` hold distinct IDs and share none (errors at `included` and `excluded`); every `covered_by` entry is in `included` (error at `excluded`); `total` is present iff `included` is non-empty (error at `total`); `completeness` is none iff nothing is included, partial if something is included and some exclusion has an empty `covered_by`, else complete (P-40 as refined by P-54; error at `completeness`) |
| `PriceFacts` | NamedTuple | `(context_tokens: int or None, batch: bool or None, region: str or None)`, each defaulting to None (unknown) |
| `PriceResolution` | NamedTuple | `(entry: PriceEntry or None, na_reason: str or None)`; exactly one is set; `na_reason` is no_price_for_model or ambiguous_price |
| `resolve_price(catalog, *, provider, model, unit, usage_time=None, run_started_at=None, facts=PriceFacts()) -> PriceResolution`; since D9 `occurred_at=` replaces `usage_time=`; (rev 21) `model: str \| None` | pure function | (rev 21, A2) `model=None` gives `no_price_for_model` whatever the catalog holds, after the argument checks and before any candidate, date or condition; `compute_cost` passes each usage's `model` and so delegates. Candidates: entries with the same `provider`, a `model` equal to the entry's `model` or one of its `aliases`, and the same `unit`, all compared exactly (case, prefix and substring never match). No candidate gives `no_price_for_model` (checked before the date, N48). A candidate set with neither `usage_time` nor `run_started_at` gives `ambiguous_price`; the price date is `usage_time` if present, else `run_started_at`, never "now" (P-36). An entry is effective at `d` iff `effective_from <= d` and (`effective_to` is None or `d < effective_to`). Conditions: `context_tokens_gt`/`_le` compare `facts.context_tokens` (gt is strict, le inclusive), `batch` compares `facts.batch`, `region` compares `facts.region` exactly; a condition on an unknown fact makes the entry undecidable and the result `ambiguous_price`; a known fact that does not match removes the entry. Two or more matching entries give `ambiguous_price`; none gives `no_price_for_model` |
| `compute_cost(usages, catalog, *, domain, record_id, created_at, context, usage_times=None, run_started_at=None, facts=PriceFacts()) -> CostRecord` | pure function | `context` is a required `decimal.Context` (omitting it is a `TypeError`); no clock, no I/O, no use of the thread's current decimal context, inputs unchanged. A usage record of another `domain` is a `ValueError` containing "domain" (N57). No usage: `usage_not_reported` for `agent_under_test`, `no_usage` for `evaluation`, `usage_record_ids` empty. Otherwise each quantity of each usage record is priced on its own through `resolve_price` (usage time from `usage_times[usage.id]`, else `run_started_at`) and the amount is the exact sum. Per quantity (a billable event is one usage record and one unit, N55): round up to `increment` (unless "none"), multiply by `price.amount / unit_quantity`, then take the larger of that and `minimum` (unless "none"). The total is rounded once, half-even at scale 18, and `rounding = {18, half_even, false}` is set iff the rounding changed the value (N56). The result is available, `basis=computed`, with a `pricing_snapshot` naming the catalog `(id, version, content_digest)` and the distinct entries used, or not_available with no amount and the reason: any `ambiguous_price` gives ambiguous_price; else any entry with `billing: "unknown"` gives `billing_rules_missing`; else unpriced units give `unit_unpriced` with `unpriced_units` listed (or `no_price_for_model` when no quantity at all was priced); a partial sum is never reported. `usage_record_ids` lists the usage records given |
| `check_pricing_snapshot(snapshot, catalog_doc) -> None` | function (P, N52) | `catalog_doc` is the catalog's JSON document (`id`, `version`, `content_digest`, optionally `entries`). The rules of `snapshot.check_pricing_catalog_ref` for `content_digest`: another catalog ID or version, another scheme or a keyed digest with another `key_ref` is a `ValueError`; the same scheme and `key_ref` with another value is `RefDigestMismatchError`. If `entries` is present, an `entry_ids` member that is not an entry ID is a `ValueError` containing "entry" |
| `summarize_costs(records, *, domain, summary_id, created_at) -> CostSummary` | pure function (P, N53) | Builds the summary. A record of another domain is a `ValueError` containing "domain". Available records are included in input order, except that an available computed record whose usage is entirely inside the usage of available reported records is excluded with reason `duplicate_of_reported` and `covered_by` naming those reported records (P-54); two available records of the same basis sharing a usage record are a `ValueError` containing "usage"; so is a partial overlap of a computed record with reported ones (N53). Not-available records are excluded with their `na_reason`. `covered_by` of an exclusion lists the included records, in input order, that share a usage record with the excluded record, if together they cover all of its usage, else it is empty; a record without usage is never covered. `total` is the exact sum of the included amounts, absent when nothing is included |
| `check_cost_summary(summary, records) -> None` | function (P, N53) | `ValueError` with a message containing one of the fragments in brackets: a record named by the summary and not supplied [record]; a named record of another domain [domain]; an included record that is not available [available]; two included records sharing a usage record [usage]; a stored total that is not the exact sum of the included amounts, or a total where none belongs [total]; an exclusion whose reason is not the record's `na_reason`, or a reason other than `duplicate_of_reported` for an available record, or `duplicate_of_reported` for a record that is not computed [reason, available or duplicate]; a `duplicate_of_reported` exclusion that does not name an included reported record [reported]; a `covered_by` that differs from the included records covering the record's usage [covered_by]; a supplied record that is neither included nor excluded [excluded]. The order of `covered_by` is not checked |
| `normalize_currency_label(label) -> str or None` | pure function | "USD" iff the label equals USD after trimming ASCII white space (space, tab, newline, carriage return, form feed, vertical tab) and ASCII case folding (A to Z only); everything else is None |
| `ContractCurrency` | NamedTuple | `(currency: str, url: str or None, retrieved_at: datetime or None)`: the currency a published API contract states for a cost field, with the citation of the field provenance table. It is a citation only if `url` is non-empty and `retrieved_at` is set |
| `ImportedCost` | NamedTuple | `(record: CostRecord, warnings: list[IngestWarning])` |
| `import_reported_cost(*, domain, usage_record_ids, reported_by, amount_raw, currency_raw, record_id, created_at, contract=None) -> ImportedCost` | pure function | `currency_raw` is the label as sent or None when the payload has none. A label that normalizes to USD gives an available reported record; if the label is not exactly "USD" the warning `currency_label_normalized` has `raw` equal to the label as sent. A canonical amount lexeme is kept; a valid decimal in another spelling ("12.50", "7.", "0.0") is converted and the warning `decimal_normalized` has `raw` equal to the lexeme (ADR 0014 Decision 1a). Any other label (including `$`, `US$`, `dollars` and every non-USD ISO code, and text that is no ISO code) gives not_available/`unsupported_currency` with `reported_raw` holding `amount_raw`, `currency_raw` and `reported_by` verbatim and `currency_source=payload`, plus the warning `unsupported_currency` whose message contains the code when the ISO 4217 data file knows it; nothing is parsed, normalized or converted. No label and no cited contract gives not_available/`currency_not_reported` with `currency_source=none` and `currency_raw` absent. No label and a cited contract uses the contract's currency with `currency_source=api_contract` (USD gives an available record, anything else `unsupported_currency` with `currency_raw` as written in the contract). An uncited contract (no URL, an empty URL or no `retrieved_at`) counts as no contract. (rev 20, N82) Never raises on the text of `currency_raw` or `amount_raw`: a lone surrogate makes the text unparseable (label `unsupported_currency`, USD amount `amount_unparseable`), the stored raw and every warning `raw` have it replaced by U+FFFD, and one `text_not_valid_unicode` warning per affected field (label first, then amount, before every other warning) carries that field's path |

### 23.2 Test map

Every SCOPE section 5 name of P1-23, P1-24 and P1-25 exists once, except `test_unevaluable_applies_when_ambiguous`, which SCOPE lists under P1-24 but which needs `resolve_price` of P1-25 and is in `test_cost_compute.py` (N46). The 206 collected tests (90 test functions, 5 of them parametrized) are:

- **P1-23, `test_usage.py` (19 collected):** `test_token_counts_int_nonneg` (6 units), `test_audio_seconds_decimal` (2), `test_fractional_tokens_rejected` (6), `test_usage_domain_stage_enum`, `test_raw_preserved`, `test_agent_domain_measured_usage_rejected`; proposed: `test_usage_quantity_shape_and_unit_set`, `test_usage_record_shape_and_required_fields`.
- **P1-24, `test_pricing.py` (61):** `test_catalog_digest_immutable`, `test_overlapping_ranges_rejected` (8 cases), `test_provenance_required`, `test_user_supplied_marked`, `test_explicit_zero_price_allowed`, `test_price_entry_requires_billing_rules`, `test_billing_parts_required_value_or_none`, `test_price_entry_rejects_non_usd` (10 codes x catalog/user_supplied x price/minimum = 40); proposed: `test_pricing_catalog_shape`, `test_price_entry_shape_and_enums`, `test_applies_when_conditions_closed_kinds_and_value_types`, `test_overlapping_ranges_only_within_one_key`, `test_price_and_minimum_nonnegative`, `test_every_non_usd_iso_code_rejected_in_prices` (every code of the vendored file), `test_pricing_models_emit_enum_not_const_and_string_amounts`.
- **P1-25, `test_cost.py` (13):** `test_basis_enum_exact`, `test_not_available_cost_has_no_basis`, `test_cost_amount_nonnegative`, `test_reported_basis_requires_reporter`, `test_computed_requires_pricing_snapshot`, `test_cost_pricing_snapshot_content_digest_named_and_verified`, `test_reported_non_usd_cost_not_available_raw_preserved`, `test_reported_raw_present_iff_currency_na_reason`, `test_reported_raw_currency_source_consistent`; proposed: `test_cost_record_shape_and_enums`, `test_pricing_snapshot_entry_ids_resolve_in_catalog`, `test_cost_rounding_fields_exact`, `test_unpriced_units_listed_only_with_unit_unpriced`.
- **P1-25, `test_cost_summary.py` (21):** `test_cost_summary_total_absent_when_none_included`, `test_completeness_rules` (11 rows), `test_cost_summary_single_usd_total_with_exclusions`, `test_usage_record_in_at_most_one_included_cost`, `test_reported_non_usd_and_computed_usd_same_usage_counted_once`, `test_available_reported_preferred_over_computed_duplicate`, `test_cost_exclusion_covered_by_names_included_records_only`, `test_completeness_ignores_covered_exclusions`; proposed: `test_cost_exclusion_shape_and_reasons`, `test_cost_summary_shape_and_enums`, `test_check_cost_summary_other_rules`.
- **P1-25, `test_cost_compute.py` (25):** `test_compute_exact`, `test_billing_increment_and_minimum_applied`, `test_compute_scale_overflow_rounds_half_even_and_records`, `test_price_resolution_uses_usage_time`, `test_price_resolution_falls_back_to_run_started_at`, `test_price_resolution_without_any_date_is_ambiguous`, `test_unevaluable_applies_when_ambiguous`, `test_no_price_not_available_never_zero`, `test_partial_units_not_available`, `test_missing_billing_rules_not_available`, `test_billing_unknown_gives_billing_rules_missing`, `test_ambiguous_match_not_available`, `test_agent_cost_not_available_without_reported_usage`, `test_recording_duration_not_accepted_as_agent_stt_usage`, `test_black_box_llm_tokens_not_available`, `test_user_supplied_price_is_computed_with_price_source`; proposed: `test_compute_multi_unit_and_multi_usage_sum`, `test_billing_increment_per_billable_event`, `test_compute_matches_independent_decimal_property` (hypothesis), `test_compute_is_pure_and_needs_an_explicit_context`, `test_exact_model_match_no_fuzzy_matching`, `test_agent_domain_computed_from_telemetry_usage`, `test_compute_cost_domain_mismatch_rejected`, `test_explicit_zero_price_gives_available_zero`, `test_resolve_price_result_shape`.
- **P1-25, `test_cost_import.py` (38):** `test_reported_currency_case_variant_normalized_with_warning`, `test_reported_currency_symbol_not_normalized`, `test_reported_cost_without_currency_not_available`, `test_documented_currency_contract_requires_citation`; proposed: `test_normalize_currency_label_accepts_usd_variants` (10), `test_normalize_currency_label_rejects_everything_else` (22), `test_import_non_usd_not_converted_parsed_or_rejected`, `test_import_usd_amount_lexeme_normalized_with_warning`.
- **Contract, `test_cost_contract.py` (2):** `test_no_float_in_cost_path`, `test_every_money_amount_schema_is_string_pattern`.
- **Entities, `test_batch4_entities.py` (27, all proposed):** the eight tests of `test_batch3_entities.py`, over `UsageRecord`, `PricingCatalog`, `PriceEntry`, `CostRecord`, `CostSummary` (registered with prefix, round trip and schema, unknown fields, schema version and ID kind, digest of the catalog, records of fact not versioned, `enum` not `const`, found by the registry walk).

Tests that also depend on batch 3: `import_reported_cost` returns `IngestWarning` objects (P1-10c), so the six tests that call it (`test_reported_currency_case_variant_normalized_with_warning`, `test_reported_currency_symbol_not_normalized`, `test_reported_cost_without_currency_not_available`, `test_documented_currency_contract_requires_citation`, `test_import_non_usd_not_converted_parsed_or_rejected`, `test_import_usd_amount_lexeme_normalized_with_warning`) need the batch 3 class in the implementation, not in the tests. Every test that uses `registered_class` or `registry.*` goes through the registry walk, which imports every module of the package, so it also fails while a batch 3 module cannot be imported; the red logs separate this (23.5).

### 23.3 Ambiguities and contradictions (listed, not guessed; the pinned reading is in 23.1). Revision 15: each item is marked Decided (owner) or Needs owner decision; revision 16: every item is decided

- **N43. No time on a usage record.** ADR 0014 Decision 15 (P-36) prices at "the usage event's time if the source reports it", but Decision 8 gives `UsageRecord` no time field, and the tests pin the field set. Pinned (P): the time is an argument, `compute_cost(..., usage_times={usage_id: datetime})` and `resolve_price(..., usage_time=)`. Owner/architect: keep this, or add an optional `occurred_at` to `UsageRecord` (then the mapping is `usage.occurred_at`, and the two `usage_times` tests change). **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A.** `UsageRecord.occurred_at: UtcDatetime?` (absent when the source does not state it, never filled with ingest time, run start or now); price date `occurred_at`, else `run_started_at`, else not_available with `ambiguous_price` (P-36 reason kept, detail D10), never now. `compute_cost` loses `usage_times`; `resolve_price` takes `occurred_at=` (D9). ADR 0014 Decisions 8 and 15, ADR 0002 Decision 4a. Test changes: 23.6 A.
- **N44. Shape of `quantities`.** The ADR says units are disjoint buckets. Pinned (P): non-empty, one quantity per unit. An empty `quantities` list has no meaning that `no_usage` does not already cover. ~~Needs owner decision~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner): option A** (non-empty, exactly one per unit; the type never sums repeats; importers merge or map to distinct units). ADR 0014 Decision 9.
- **N45. Seconds.** The ADR says seconds are a `DecimalString`, not that they are >= 0. Pinned (P): negative rejected. A zero quantity is accepted, but what increment and minimum do to a zero quantity (a minimum charge for no usage?) is undecided and untested. ~~Needs owner decision~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner):** (a) negative seconds rejected, no negative quantity in any unit; (b) a zero quantity costs exactly 0 and no minimum or increment applies. ADR 0014 Decisions 7, 9, 15. Follow-up: N63 (23.8; decided in revision 17).
- **N46. A misplaced test and the facts of conditions.** SCOPE lists `test_unevaluable_applies_when_ambiguous` under P1-24, which cannot make it pass without `resolve_price` (P1-25). The ADR does not say how "known usage" yields `context_tokens` (do cache buckets count?), and `batch` and `region` are not on `UsageRecord` at all. Pinned (P): the caller passes `PriceFacts`; the cost path never derives them from the quantities. **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A.** The caller passes `PriceFacts`; an unknown fact that an entry's condition needs gives `ambiguous_price`, so the cost is not_available; `UsageRecord` is not widened. `test_unevaluable_applies_when_ambiguous` stays in `test_cost_compute.py`; SCOPE section 5 moves it from P1-24 to P1-25. ADR 0014 Decision 15.
- **N47. Catalog contents.** ADR 0014 calls `PriceEntry` an entity (`pce`, with an ID kept across catalog versions, P-24) and the catalog `{id, version, entries, published_at}`. Pinned (P): the entries are embedded entities, so each carries `schema_version`, `created_at`, `metadata` inside the catalog's digest domain. ADR 0006 Decision 5 removes `schema_version` only at the top level (the snapshot domain removes it at named nested paths); an embedded entry's `schema_version` therefore changes the catalog digest across schema versions. Owner/architect: add a nested exclusion for entries (as for the snapshot), or keep entries as value objects with an ID. Also undecided: empty `entries`; two entries of different keys that match the same model through an alias (the catalog accepts it, the resolver answers `ambiguous_price`); P-24 (an unchanged entry keeps its ID across catalog versions) has no SCOPE test and no function, so none was written. **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A.** `PriceEntry` is a value object inside `PricingCatalog` (no `id`, `schema_version`, `created_at`, `metadata`, `metadata_flags`) with a stable entry identifier; the catalog digest covers prices and rules, with no per-entry entity fields (read as: every entry field, D4); empty `entries` rejected; entries of different keys that match one usage (the alias cases) are accepted by the catalog and resolve to `ambiguous_price`; overlapping ranges under one key stay rejected (A-25, N49). Prefix `pce` withdrawn (ADR 0001). ADR 0014 Decisions 10 and 11, ADR 0006 Decision 5, ADR 0009 Decision 5. ~~Still open~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner):** the identifier is `entry_key: MachineKey` (N61, option B); P-24 is checked by `pricing.check_catalog_succession` (option A). Follow-up: N65 (23.8; decided in revision 17).
- **N48. Range semantics and the order of "no price" and "no date".** The ADR does not say whether `effective_to` is inclusive. Pinned (P): half-open `[from, to)`, adjacent ranges do not overlap, `effective_to` later than `effective_from`. P-36 says a missing date gives `ambiguous_price`; when no entry matches provider, model and unit at all the date is irrelevant. Pinned (P): `no_price_for_model` first, then the date. **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A.** Half-open `[effective_from, effective_to)`, `effective_to` optional and later than `effective_from`; no candidate on provider, model and unit gives `no_price_for_model` before any date check. ADR 0014 Decisions 11 and 15.
- **N49. `applies_when` details.** The value types per kind (P), `applies_when` compared as a set in the overlap key (P), and an empty list, a repeated kind, and an entry with one false and one unknown condition (the tests do not pin it; the oracle treats a known false as removing the entry) are not in the ADR. **Decided (revision 15, owner): accepted as pinned** (values typed per kind; overlap key with `applies_when` as a set). ~~Still open~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner): accepted as recommended** (`[]` rejected; repeated kind rejected; all conditions must hold, so a known false removes the entry).
- **N50. Provenance.** ADR 0003 lists no set for `provenance.kind` (pinned open). `provenance.url` is not covered by the URL credential rules of ADR 0007 Decision 6b, so a presigned or tokenised price-page URL is accepted today. Whether `note` is required for a user-supplied price converted from another currency (ADR 0014 Decision 11 says the note states the rate and date) is not enforced. Owner: decide the URL rule (recommendation: apply Decision 6b). **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A** for the URL: `provenance.url` runs the ADR 0007 URL credential rules (userinfo, the 25 names, the key patterns), with an error that names the rule and part (ADR 0007 Decision 6e). ~~Still open~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner):** URL syntax, open `kind`, `note` required for `user_supplied` (accepted as recommended); N62 option A (the three pricing models hide). Follow-up: N66 (23.8; decided in revision 17).
- **N51. Cross-field rules of `CostRecord` that the ADR leaves open.** Pinned (P): `reported_by` only on basis reported, `pricing_snapshot` and `rounding` only on basis computed (`rounding` only on an available record), `entry_ids` non-empty and without repeats, `unpriced_units` non-empty iff `unit_unpriced` and without repeats. Not pinned: whether `usage_record_ids` may be empty for an available reported record (P-54 coverage needs usage IDs), whether its entries must be unique, and whether an `unsupported_currency` record may carry a `currency_raw` that the importer would have normalized to USD. **Decided (revision 15, owner): accepted as pinned** (`reported_by` only on basis reported; `pricing_snapshot` and `rounding` only on basis computed; entry identifiers non-empty and distinct; `unpriced_units` non-empty iff `unit_unpriced`, no repeats). ~~Still open~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner): accepted as recommended** (empty `usage_record_ids` only on an available reported record among available records; repeats rejected; stored labels that contradict Decision 12c rejected). Follow-up: N67 (23.8; decided in revision 17).
- **N52. Verifying the snapshot.** ADR 0014 Decision 12 says the snapshot digest is "compared by the rules of `snapshot.check_pricing_catalog_ref`", which takes a `PricingCatalogRef`. Pinned (P): a function `cost.check_pricing_snapshot(snapshot, catalog_doc)` with the same rules, and the check that every `entry_ids` member is an entry of the catalog (not in the ADR). The bundle seam (`bundle.check_bundle_consistency`) is exercised by passing a reference built from the snapshot's dump (`catalog_id` as `id`, `version`, `content_digest`); the P1-14 bundle must do this mapping. **Decided (revision 15, owner):** the names `cost.check_pricing_snapshot`, `summarize_costs` and `check_cost_summary` are kept, with the entry check. ADR 0014 Decision 16b.
- **N53. Building and checking a summary.** ADR 0014 Decision 16a states the invariants but names no function that produces the summary, and N22 makes cross-record rules bundle-level checks. Pinned (P): `summarize_costs` (with the precedence of P-54 and an explicit `summary_id` and `created_at`, because it is pure) and `check_cost_summary`, with the message fragments of 23.1. Undecided (the oracle raises, no test): a computed record that shares only part of its usage with reported records, a record with no usage in the vacuous "all usage covered" sense (pinned: never covered), and the order of `included` and `excluded` (pinned: input order). **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A** for the partial overlap: the computed record is excluded whole (no splitting), the summary is partial with a reason; reported preferred over computed. Applied with the new `CostExclusion.reason` member `overlaps_reported` and an empty `covered_by` (D8). This replaces the oracle's `ValueError`. ADR 0014 Decision 16b. ~~Still open~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner): accepted as recommended** (never covered; input order kept, not checked).
- **N54. Which reason when causes mix.** ADR 0014 Decision 13 gives each cause its reason but not a precedence. The tests pin single causes and two mixes (a priced unit plus an unpriced one gives `unit_unpriced`; a priced unit plus an `unknown`-billing unit gives `billing_rules_missing`). Not pinned: ambiguous plus unpriced, billing unknown plus unpriced, and a model that has entries only for other units than the usage's (`no_price_for_model` or `unit_unpriced`). The oracle's order is ambiguous_price, billing_rules_missing, unit_unpriced, no_price_for_model. **Decided (revision 15, owner): accepted** with the order no usage, `no_price_for_model`, `billing_rules_missing`, `unit_unpriced`, `ambiguous_price`, first applicable only (the reverse of the oracle's for `ambiguous_price`). Applied per quantity (D11): `no_price_for_model` when every quantity resolves to it, so a model priced only for other units is `no_price_for_model`. ADR 0014 Decision 13a.
- **N55. Billable event.** ADR 0014 Decision 11 says the minimum is "per billable event" and Decision 15 computes "per billable event". Pinned (P): one usage record and one unit; increment and minimum apply to each, not to the sum of several usage records. A usage record with two units whose entries both have a minimum is untested. **Decided (revision 15, owner): accepted** (one usage record and one unit; increment and minimum per quantity). ADR 0014 Decision 13b.
- **N56. Where P-35 rounds.** Pinned (P): once, on the record's total, not per event. **Decided (revision 15, owner): accepted** (once, on the record's total). ADR 0014 Decision 15.
- **N57. Domain mismatch.** The ADR gives a cost record one `domain`; mixing usage of both domains in one computation is pinned as a `ValueError` containing "domain". This is also what makes "a recording's duration is never accepted as the agent's STT usage" hold in code: a measured evaluation-domain usage cannot be passed as the agent's. **Decided (revision 15, owner): accepted** (`ValueError` containing "domain"). ADR 0014 Decision 15.
- **N58. The float scan.** `test_no_float_in_cost_path` reads the source of exactly `usage`, `pricing` and `cost`. A helper module that holds arithmetic would escape it; the pin is "the three modules hold the cost path". **Decided (revision 15, owner): accepted, widened:** the scan covers `usage`, `pricing`, `cost` and the package modules they import for amounts (D12). ADR 0014 Decision 15a.
- **N59. The importer.** ADR 0014 Decision 1a says a source number that cannot be normalized "stays raw (for example in `ReportedCostRaw.amount_raw`)", but Decision 12 allows `reported_raw` only with `unsupported_currency` or `currency_not_reported`. So a reported USD amount that is no decimal ("1e3", "1,000", "abc") or is negative has no valid record: contradiction. The oracle raises; no test pins it. Owner: choose an `na_reason` (for example a new `amount_not_parseable`), or reject at import. Also undecided and untested: a payload label together with a contract that disagrees (the oracle uses the payload label, per Decision 12b "a label sent in the payload has `currency_source=payload`"), an empty label, Unicode white space such as U+00A0 (the tests pin ASCII white space and ASCII folding only; the long s, full-width letters, a zero-width space and a combining mark are pinned as not USD), the `raw` and `path` of the `unsupported_currency` warning, and any warning for `currency_not_reported`. **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A.** New `na_reason` `amount_unparseable` (amount not a valid decimal after lexical normalization, or negative): not_available, `reported_raw` verbatim, warning `amount_unparseable`; no lenient parsing, no rejection of the import. Edge cases, all accepted: a payload label disagreeing with a cited contract: the payload label wins, the cost is not_available, raw kept; an empty label: `currency_not_reported`; only ASCII white space is trimmed, other Unicode white space or invisible characters make the label not USD (not_available, warning); `currency_not_reported` always records a warning. ADR 0014 Decisions 1a, 12 and 12c. ~~Still open~~ **Decided (revision 16, owner):** (a) option A, `currency_conflict` with the warning `currency_contract_conflict`; (b) `raw` the label as sent, `path` the optional `source_path` argument. Follow-ups: N68, N69 (23.8; decided in revision 17).
- **N60. No-echo.** ADR 0007 Decision 8 (b) does not list any cost model. The batch 3 mutant list (Q18) noted that the `Money` currency message quotes the rejected code and that "the P1-25 no-echo test must cover it". The ADR text does not require it, `ReportedCostRaw` and `UsageRecord.raw` hold provider payloads (not obviously personal data), and the ISO error message deliberately names the code (Decision 3). No no-echo test was written. Owner/staff-reviewer: decide whether any cost model hides its input. **Decided (revision 15, owner): option A.** `UsageRecord`, `ReportedCostRaw` and `CostRecord` hide their input (N42 rule); `PricingCatalog`, `PriceEntry`, `CostSummary`, the other cost value objects and `Money` do not. ADR 0007 Decision 8 (b). **Collision:** with N50, ADR 0007 Decision 8 (a) requires `PriceProvenance`, `PriceEntry` and `PricingCatalog` to hide (N62, 23.7 item 10). **Revision 16: N62 decided by the owner, option A:** the three pricing models hide; this part of N60 is superseded.

### 23.4 What the tests do not cover (so it is not mistaken for coverage)

- Tier degradation (`test_deg_agent_cost_not_available_at_t0`, `test_deg_eval_cost_computed_all_tiers`) needs fake T0 to T2 agents, a Run and the evaluator; mappings F1 and F2 (`test_f1_llm_usage_to_usage_record`, `test_f1_cache_read_bucket_disjoint`, `test_f2_cost_details_reported_by_telemetry_platform`) need captures (P1-28, P1-29, P1-30).
- Bundle rules over cost records: a usage record belongs to its run through `run_id` only; run membership, unique IDs per bundle, the summary against the bundle, `check_observed_tier` (a usage record with `domain=agent_under_test` and `source=telemetry` is telemetry evidence), and `bundle.check_bundle_consistency` over cost documents all wait for `RunBundle` (P1-14).
- Sealing, redaction and export of `UsageRecord.raw` and `ReportedCostRaw` (P1-15, P1-26): untested.
- `test_run_started_at_optional_utc` is a Run test (`test_run.py`); the cost tests receive `run_started_at` as an argument.
- P-24 (entry ID stability across catalog versions): no SCOPE test; see N47. (Revision 16: covered by `test_entry_identifier_stable_across_catalog_versions` through `check_catalog_succession`, 23.6 group O.)
- A-14 as an export adversarial case and the CHANGELOG classification of adding a currency (ADR 0014 Decision 5) are docs and export tests.
- Python 3.15, Postgres, Linux and native-speaker reviews were not run; the oracle was written by the same agent as the tests.

### 23.5 Validation (qa, 2026-10-23)

- Red logs: `docs/phase1/evidence/red/batch4-cost/` (header: date, command, versions, `uv.lock` sha256), Python 3.11, 3.12, 3.13 and 3.14. `new-tests-repo-state` is the repository as it was during the run (batch 3 in progress, no cost modules). `new-tests-batch3-oracle-base` runs the same tests against a complete batch 3 oracle that has no cost modules, so every failure there is a cost failure. `oracle-green-*` runs the tests against the oracle with cost modules (206 passed on all four versions; the full suite 1063 passed and 5 failed, the five being the known gate-bound and P1-20a tests: `test_every_entity_has_registered_prefix` (rbnd, exp), `test_opaque_id_fields_are_exactly_the_adr_allowlist`, `test_value_objects_have_no_entity_fields` (SealedPayload, RedactionRecord) and the two policy-document tests). With the oracle, `test_versioned_entity_set_matches_adr` turns green (PricingCatalog) and no cost model breaks a gate-bound walk (typed IDs, value objects without entity fields, secret names, `enum` not `const`).
- Mutants: `mutants.txt` in the same directory. 228 mutants over the oracle (usage 28, pricing 38, cost records, resolver and computation 83, summaries 12, summary and snapshot checks 18, importer 27, float scan 4, type and closed-set looseness 18), each run with `pytest -x` over the eight new files on Python 3.13: 223 caught, 5 survived. Survivors: `S08` (a computed record sharing only part of its usage with reported records: the ADR is silent, N53); `S12` (equivalent: an empty-usage record has no covering record either way); `K09` (redundant: the `covered_by` basis check catches the same summary); `K16` (equivalent: with two registered schemes a scheme difference always shows as a `key_ref` difference); `I04` (Unicode white space is not pinned, N59).

### 23.6 Revision 15, completed in revision 16: final, unconditional qa change list (schema-architect, 2026-10-23)

The owner decided N43 and N46 to N60 in chat, one at a time (decision log "Batch 4 cost decisions"; 23.3 marks each). The architect edited no test and no source file. **Revision 16 (2026-10-23): the owner decided all ten items of 23.7 and accepted D4 to D15 (decision log "Batch 4 follow-ups"). This list is now final and unconditional: the items that waited for N61, N62 or a remainder are rewritten below, and groups M to T add the tests of the revision 16 decisions.** The only cases not asserted are those of the new findings N63 to N69 (23.8); each group names them, and no test asserts either answer to them. Groups are lettered A to L; the details D4 to D15 continue the numbering of 22.12 (D1 to D3). Names are SCOPE section 5 names (revision 15 lists every batch 4 name qa proposed as binding, plus the new ones below). "Meaning extended, name kept" means the binding name stays and the body checks more.

**Details fixed while applying the decisions (revision 16: all twelve accepted by the owner as written; D5 with the six ASCII white-space characters):**
- D4 (N47) "The catalog digest covers prices and rules only" is read as "no per-entry entity fields": the generic domain covers every entry field, including provenance and the entry identifier, so altering them under one `(id, version)` is a `DigestMismatch` (ADR 0006 Decision 5).
- D5 (N59) "Only ASCII spaces are trimmed" is read as the six ASCII white-space characters qa pinned (U+0009 to U+000D and U+0020), because the question contrasted ASCII with other Unicode white space. Accepted by the owner (revision 16): the six characters; `test_normalize_currency_label_accepts_usd_variants` keeps its tab, newline and carriage-return cases.
- D6 (N59) A label that is empty, or ASCII white space only, counts as no label: `currency_not_reported` without a cited contract, the contract's currency with one (ADR 0014 Decision 12b). The `currency_not_reported` warning's `raw` is the label as sent.
- D7 (N59) The amount is parsed only once the currency is USD (ADR 0014 Decision 12 says a non-USD amount is never parsed), so `amount_unparseable` goes with `currency_source` `payload` or `api_contract`, and `"abc"` with label `EUR` is `unsupported_currency`, with no label `currency_not_reported`. Decision 1a normalizes only trailing zeros and a trailing point, so `"+1"`, `"-0"`, `" 12.5"` and `"012.5"` are `amount_unparseable`.
- D8 (N53) The new `CostExclusion.reason` member is named `overlaps_reported`.
- D9 (N43) `resolve_price(catalog, *, provider, model, unit, occurred_at=None, run_started_at=None, facts=PriceFacts())`: the keyword `usage_time=` is renamed after the field.
- D10 (N43) No price date gives `ambiguous_price`, the P-36 reason; no new reason.
- D11 (N54) The precedence is applied per quantity: `no_price_for_model` iff every quantity resolves to it (so a model priced only for other units, or only at other dates, is `no_price_for_model`); `unit_unpriced` iff some but not all do.
- D12 (N58) The float scan covers `usage`, `pricing`, `cost` and `primitives`; every `tracer_evalschema` module that the three import directly must be scanned or named in an explicit list of modules without amounts in the test.
- D13 (N47) `pce` is not reserved after its withdrawal; ~~`validate_new_prefix("pce")` rejects it anyway (one edit from `per`)~~. **Corrected in revision 18 (qa finding 1, auto-approved):** `validate_new_prefix("pce")` returns `None` (accepted): the Levenshtein distance from `pce` to `per` is 2 and `pce` is no anagram of a registered prefix; the prefix is simply unreserved (ADR 0001 Decision 4).
- D14 (N47) "Overlapping entries accepted by the catalog" is read as entries of different keys that can match one usage (aliases, condition sets that can both hold); overlapping ranges under one key stay rejected (A-25, N49's key).
- D15 (N59) A payload label and a cited contract agree when the label normalizes (P-52) to the contract's currency.

**A. N43, `UsageRecord.occurred_at`**
1. `_support_b4.py`: `usage_doc`, `make_usage` and `usage_for` accept `occurred_at` (absent by default); `compute` no longer forwards `usage_times`; the `_resolve` helper of `test_cost_compute.py` passes `occurred_at=` instead of `usage_time=` (D9).
2. `test_usage.py::test_usage_record_shape_and_required_fields`: the expected field set gains `occurred_at`, optional and not in `required`. Specification change by N43 (the docstring's "no time field" goes); every other assertion kept.
3. New `test_usage.py::test_usage_record_occurred_at_optional_utc` (P1-23): absent and `None` accepted; a `UtcDatetime` round-trips in canonical form; naive, numeric, date-only and more than six fractional digits rejected at `occurred_at` by pydantic and JSON Schema.
4. `test_cost_compute.py::test_price_resolution_uses_usage_time` (name kept): each usage carries its time as `occurred_at` instead of `usage_times={usage.id: ...}`; `_resolve(..., occurred_at=...)`. Every assertion kept. Add: `compute_cost(..., usage_times=...)` is a `TypeError` (the argument is withdrawn).
5. `::test_price_resolution_falls_back_to_run_started_at` (name kept): the `timed` usage carries `occurred_at`.
6. `::test_price_resolution_without_any_date_is_ambiguous` (name kept): the `dated` usage carries `occurred_at`; reason stays `ambiguous_price` (D10).
7. `test_cost_compute.py` module docstring: the two signatures as in D9 and without `usage_times`.

**B. N47, `PriceEntry` is a value object; N61 (revision 16, option B): its identifier is `entry_key`**
1. `_support_b4.py`: `entry_doc` drops `meta("pce")` (no `id`, `schema_version`, `created_at`, `metadata`) and carries `entry_key`, a `MachineKey` chosen by the test (for example `"acme-stt-audio-2026-10"`); `ENTITY_SPECS4` loses the `("pricing", "PriceEntry", "pce", False)` row; `doc4` loses the `PriceEntry` builder; `snapshot_doc` reads `e.entry_key` instead of `e.id` and writes `entry_keys` instead of `entry_ids`.
2. `test_batch4_entities.py`: the eight functions run over four entities (UsageRecord, PricingCatalog, CostRecord, CostSummary) and `BATCH4_VALUE_OBJECTS` gains `PriceEntry`, so `test_batch4_entity_is_registered_with_its_prefix`, `test_batch4_entity_roundtrip_and_schema_agree`, `test_batch4_entity_rejects_unknown_fields`, `test_batch4_entity_checks_schema_version_and_id_kind` and `test_batch4_records_of_fact_are_not_versioned` lose their `PriceEntry` case, while `test_batch4_models_emit_enum_not_const` and `test_batch4_models_are_all_found_by_the_registry_walk` check `PriceEntry` as a value object (in `value_object_classes()`, not in `entity_classes()`). Specification change by N47.
3. `test_pricing.py::test_price_entry_shape_and_enums`: the field set is `entry_key` plus the twelve entry fields and no entity field (`entry_key` required, a `MachineKey`: `""`, `"bad key"` and a non-string rejected at `entry_key`); `PriceEntry` is not an `EntityBase` subclass. Specification change by N47.
4. `::test_pricing_catalog_shape` (meaning extended): `entries=[]` rejected at `entries` by pydantic and JSON Schema (`minItems: 1`); the duplicate case uses a repeated `entry_key` instead of a repeated `id`.
5. `::test_catalog_digest_immutable` (meaning extended): changing an entry's `provenance.retrieved_at` or its `entry_key` without a new digest is a `DigestMismatch` (D4).
6. Entry `.id` becomes `.entry_key` and `pricing_snapshot.entry_ids` becomes `pricing_snapshot.entry_keys` in `test_cost_compute.py` (the assertions on `pricing_snapshot.entry_keys` and `direct.entry` in `test_compute_exact`, `test_price_resolution_uses_usage_time`, `test_unevaluable_applies_when_ambiguous`, `test_user_supplied_price_is_computed_with_price_source`, `test_explicit_zero_price_gives_available_zero`) and in `test_cost.py::test_computed_requires_pricing_snapshot`.
7. `test_cost.py::test_computed_requires_pricing_snapshot`: the field is `entry_keys`. The case `("entry_ids", [str(ids.UsageRecordId.new())])` (a wrong-kind typed ID) has no meaning for an author key: replace it with `("entry_keys", [""])`, `("entry_keys", ["bad key"])`, `("entry_keys", [])` and `("entry_keys", ["k1", "k1"])`, each rejected at `entry_keys` by pydantic and JSON Schema (`minItems: 1`, `uniqueItems: true`, the `MachineKey` pattern); add that a snapshot carrying `entry_ids` is rejected as an unknown field (like `digest`, O6). Specification change by N61, no assertion removed. **Rename (SCOPE revision 16):** `test_pricing_snapshot_entry_ids_resolve_in_catalog` becomes `test_pricing_snapshot_entry_keys_resolve_in_catalog`; its body uses `entry_keys` and checks that a key naming no entry of the catalog document is a `ValueError` containing "entry"; the old name must not exist.
8. `test_ids.py`, `ADR_PREFIXES`: remove `"pce": "PriceEntry"` (26 entries). The expected sets of `test_prefix_registry_frozen_matches_adr`, `test_id_roundtrip_prefixed` (registry set equality), `test_frozen_registry_confusable_pairs_are_the_known_five` (registry equality; the five pairs are unchanged) and of every test that iterates `ID_PREFIX_NAMES` lose `pce`; `test_every_entity_has_registered_prefix` then expects 26 prefixes and 26 `EntityBase` subclasses, with no `PriceEntry` among them. `VALUE_OBJECT_NAMES` gains `"PriceEntry"`, so `test_value_objects_have_no_entity_fields` requires it to exist without `id`, `schema_version`, `created_at` or `metadata`. **Why this is a specification change and not a weakening:** these tables are deliberately literal copies of ADR 0001 Decisions 4 and 8 ("do not generate this from the implementation"), and the owner's N47 decision changed those decisions. No assertion is removed: one class moves from the entity list to the value-object list, where the stricter "no entity fields" rule applies, and `test_versioned_entity_set_matches_adr` is unchanged (`PriceEntry` was never versioned).
9. `test_ids.py`, `OPAQUE_ID_FIELDS` and `NON_ENTITY_ID_FIELDS` (`test_opaque_id_fields_are_exactly_the_adr_allowlist`, `test_every_id_reference_field_has_registered_prefix`): **no change** (N61, option B). `entry_key` and `entry_keys` are not `*_id`/`*_ids` names, so neither walk sees them and ADR 0001 I12's allowlist stays exactly `KeyRef.key_id`, `ToolCallEvent.external_call_id`, `Run.caller_external_id`. Both tests stay as they are.
10. `test_cost_compute.py::test_ambiguous_match_not_available` (unchanged) already pins D14; no change.

**C. N46, N48, N55, N56, N57:** no test change. SCOPE section 5 lists `test_unevaluable_applies_when_ambiguous` under P1-25 (N46).

**D. N50, provenance URL**
1. New `test_pricing.py::test_price_provenance_url_rejects_credentials` (P1-24): rejected at `provenance`/`url`, by `PriceProvenance` alone and inside `PriceEntry` and `PricingCatalog`: userinfo; credential names in the query with case, percent-encoding and `-`/`_` variants (`X-Amz-Signature`, `%74oken`, `access-token`, `x_api_key`); a Decision 5a pattern in the path and in a query value, as written and percent-encoded. Accepted: a plain `https` price page, `?tokenizer=1`, a 32+ character opaque path segment. The message names the rule and the URL part. Revision 16 (N62): no part of the URL (scheme, host, path, query name or value, the pattern match) appears in `str(error)`, `errors(include_input=False)` or their `ctx`, validated as `PriceProvenance`, `PriceEntry` and `PricingCatalog`.

New `test_cost_summary.py::test_computed_partially_overlapping_reported_excluded_not_split` (P1-25): usage `u1`, `u2`; reported available `R` on `u1`; computed available `C` on `u1`, `u2`. `summarize_costs` gives `included=[R]`, `total=R.amount`, `excluded=[{ref: C, reason: overlaps_reported, covered_by: []}]`, `completeness=partial`; `check_cost_summary` accepts it and rejects `overlaps_reported` on a fully covered computed record (that is `duplicate_of_reported`), on a computed record that shares no usage with an included reported record, and on a reported or not-available record.
2. `test_cost_summary.py::test_cost_exclusion_shape_and_reasons` (meaning extended): the reason set gains `overlaps_reported`, `amount_unparseable` and (revision 16) `currency_conflict`.

**F. N54, reason precedence**
1. New `test_cost_compute.py::test_not_available_reason_precedence` (P1-25), each case one record: every quantity unpriced, also a model priced only for another unit, gives `no_price_for_model` (D11); unknown billing plus an unpriced unit gives `billing_rules_missing` with empty `unpriced_units`; unknown billing plus an ambiguous unit gives `billing_rules_missing`; an unpriced unit plus an ambiguous unit gives `unit_unpriced` listing only the unpriced unit; a priced unit plus an ambiguous unit gives `ambiguous_price`; no usage gives `no_usage` (evaluation) and `usage_not_reported` (agent) whatever the catalog. The existing single-cause and two-mix tests agree with the order and are unchanged.

**M. N44 and N45 (a), quantities (revision 16)**
1. No test change. `test_usage_record_shape_and_required_fields` (`quantities=[]` and two quantities of one unit rejected at `quantities`), `test_token_counts_int_nonneg` and `test_audio_seconds_decimal` (a negative count or `"-1"` rejected at `count`) already assert the decided rules; qa may drop their "P reading" docstring labels (no behaviour change). How an importer merges repeated quantities is N64 (23.8); no importer test exists for it.

**N. N45 (b), zero quantity (revision 16)**
1. New `test_cost_compute.py::test_zero_quantity_costs_zero_without_minimum` (P1-25): (i) one usage with `audio_input_seconds: "0"`, priced by an entry with `increment: "15"` and a `minimum` of `"0.01"` USD, and `requests: 1` priced by another entry without a minimum: the amount is exactly the `requests` amount (the zero quantity adds 0, neither increment nor minimum applied); (ii) one usage with `input_tokens: 1000` and `cache_read_input_tokens: 0`, both units priced, the cache entry with a minimum: the amount is exactly the `input_tokens` amount; (iii) one usage whose only quantities are zero, every unit priced by an entry with `BillingRules`: `status=available`, `basis=computed`, amount `"0"`, no `rounding`. Not asserted (N63): which keys `entry_keys` lists for a zero quantity, and any case in which a zero quantity's unit has no entry, an `"unknown"` billing or an ambiguous match.

**O. N47 remainder, `pricing.check_catalog_succession` (revision 16)**
1. New `test_pricing.py::test_entry_identifier_stable_across_catalog_versions` (P1-24): `check_catalog_succession(previous, next)` with two versions of one catalog (same `id`, `next.version = previous.version + 1`). Accepted (returns `None`): every entry unchanged under its key; an entry removed; a new key with new content; a changed price under a new key while the old key is gone. Rejected with a `ValueError` whose message contains "entry" and the key: a key of `previous` reused in `next` with a different `price`, and separately with a different `unit_quantity`, `billing`, `applies_when`, `effective_from`, `unit` or `model`; an entry of `next` identical in every field to an entry of `previous` but under a new key. The message contains no provenance URL. Pure: both arguments dump equal before and after the call, and the function takes no clock or context. Not asserted (N65): a change only in `provenance`, only in `aliases` or only in `effective_to` (closing an open range); two catalogs with different `id`s; a `next.version` that is not later.

**P. N49 remainder, `applies_when` (revision 16)**
1. `test_pricing.py::test_applies_when_conditions_closed_kinds_and_value_types` (meaning extended, name kept): `applies_when=[]` is rejected at `applies_when` by pydantic and JSON Schema (`minItems: 1`), while an omitted field is accepted; two conditions of one kind (two `region`, two `context_tokens_gt`, two `batch`) are rejected at `applies_when` by pydantic. JSON Schema cannot state "kinds distinct", so this is a code-only rule: qa records it the way staff-reviewer settles N12/N38 (code-only rule or `KNOWN_SCHEMA_GAPS` entry); the test asserts the pydantic rejection.
2. New `test_cost_compute.py::test_applies_when_conditions_all_must_hold` (P1-25), through `resolve_price` with one entry carrying `batch: true` and `region: "in"` plus, where stated, a second entry: facts `batch=False, region=None` remove the entry (a known false wins over an unknown), so with no other candidate the result is `no_price_for_model`, and with an unconditional second candidate it is that entry; `batch=None, region="in"` gives `ambiguous_price`; `batch=True, region="in"` gives the entry; `batch=True, region="us"` removes it; `batch=True, region=None` gives `ambiguous_price`. A band `context_tokens_gt: 128000` plus `context_tokens_le: 1000000` matches 128001 and 1000000 and not 128000 or 1000001.

**Q. N50 remainder, provenance (revision 16)**
1. New `test_pricing.py::test_price_provenance_url_syntax` (P1-24): accepted: `https://example.com/pricing`, `http://example.com/p?model=x`, `https://example.com/pricing#audio-models`. Rejected at `provenance`/`url`, by `PriceProvenance` alone and inside `PriceEntry` and `PricingCatalog`, by pydantic: a relative reference (`/pricing`, `example.com/pricing`), the schemes `ftp`, `file`, `ws`, `s3`, an uppercase scheme (`HTTPS://example.com`), no host (`https:///pricing`), white space or a control character, a non-ASCII character, and a fragment holding a Decision 5a credential pattern as written and percent-encoded. No part of the URL appears in any error form (N62). Not asserted (N66): a fragment `#access_token=abc` without a pattern match, and loopback or private hosts.
2. New `test_pricing.py::test_user_supplied_price_requires_provenance_note` (P1-24): an entry with `price_source=user_supplied` and no `provenance.note` is rejected at `provenance`/`note` by pydantic (the JSON Schema half follows staff-reviewer's ruling on N12/N38; batch 3 emits `if`/`then` for its presence rules, 22.13 item 11); with a note it is accepted; a `catalog` entry without a note is accepted. **Implementation follows these items** (schema-architect, not qa): `ids.py` loses `pce` and `PriceEntryId`; `PriceEntry` derives from the value-object base with `entry_key`, and `PricingSnapshot.entry_keys` (N61); `PriceProvenance`, `PriceEntry` and `PricingCatalog` hide their input (N62); `UsageRecord.occurred_at`; the importer steps of ADR 0014 Decision 12c with `currency_conflict` and `source_path`; `pricing.check_catalog_succession` (Decision 10a); Decisions 9, 11, 12, 13a, 15, 15a and 16b as revised in revision 16.
